  POST   /sandboxes                -- E2B-shaped (NewSandbox -> Sandbox), boots a real VM and its own process.Process gRPC listener (port in the response's processPort)
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/exec     -- NOT E2B's envd protocol; a real exec_in_guest (simpler than grpcurl-ing processPort)
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/connect  -- attach to an existing sandbox; what the E2B SDK's Sandbox.connect() calls
  DELETE /sandboxes/{id}          -- stop the VM and its process.Process listener
  PUT    /sandboxes/{id}/network  -- replace a running sandbox's egress rules
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/pause    -- suspend to disk; connect or resume brings it back,          and with autoResume so does a request through the proxy
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/fork     -- checkpoint in place, start `count` copies
  GET    /sandboxes/{id}/network/decisions -- NOT E2B's; the gateway's audit log

hv2-sandboxd: listening on 0.0.0.0:49967, capacity 16, cluster node owned-private-secondary
  POST   /sandboxes                -- E2B-shaped (NewSandbox -> Sandbox), boots a real VM and its own process.Process gRPC listener (port in the response's processPort)
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/exec     -- NOT E2B's envd protocol; a real exec_in_guest (simpler than grpcurl-ing processPort)
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/connect  -- attach to an existing sandbox; what the E2B SDK's Sandbox.connect() calls
  DELETE /sandboxes/{id}          -- stop the VM and its process.Process listener
  PUT    /sandboxes/{id}/network  -- replace a running sandbox's egress rules
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/pause    -- suspend to disk; connect or resume brings it back,          and with autoResume so does a request through the proxy
  POST   /sandboxes/{id}/fork     -- checkpoint in place, start `count` copies
  GET    /sandboxes/{id}/network/decisions -- NOT E2B's; the gateway's audit log
sandboxes get a network, egress default Deny for a sandbox that configures none
